    SELECT p.id, p.brand, p.category_id, p.color, p.created_at, p.description, p.image_url, p.is_active, p.name, p.price, p.size, p.stock_quantity, p.updated_at
    FROM public.products AS p
    ORDER BY p.created_at DESC
    LIMIT @__p_1 OFFSET @__p_0
) AS p0
INNER JOIN public.categories AS c ON p0.category_id = c.id
ORDER BY p0.created_at DESC
